  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Nebuchadnezzar II, leader of Babylon (Player 6).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Babylon
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Bowman, Replacing Archer (Requires technology: Calendar)
        - Unique Unit: Sabum Kibitum, Replacing Spearman (Requires technology: Bronze Working)
        - Unique Building: Walls of Babylon, Replacing Walls (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Unique Building: Etemenanki, Replacing National Monument (Requires technology: Drama and Poetry)
        - Civilization Ability: Receive a Free Great Scientist when you discover Writing, and Great Scientists are earned 50% faster than normal. Investing Gold in Buildings reduces their Production cost by an additional 15%.
      - Leader: Nebuchadnezzar II
      - PlayerID: 6
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Satellites: Allow you to build the Apollo Program, a project necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: The Internet, Lasers
      - Radar: Allows you to build the Heavy Bomber, a deadly air Unit capable of delivering death from afar, and the Fighter, an air Unit designed to wrest control of the skies from enemy aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Advanced Ballistics, Mobile Tactics
      - Alternative Energy: Allows you to build the Solar Plant, a building which may only be built in Cities on or next to Desert, and increases Production. Also allows you to build the Recycling Center, which provides bonus Aluminum.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics
      
      ## Policies
      - Spaceflight Pioneers (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Order): Spaceflight Pioneers: Gain a free Great Engineer and Great Scientist. May finish Spaceship parts with Great Engineers, and expending a Great Person grants 100 Science, scaling with Era. Hurrying Production with Great Engineers 50% more effective.
      - Socialist Realism (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Order): Socialist Realism: Unlocks the Propaganda Process (converts 15% of Production into Tourism). +25% Tourism to other Civilizations following Order. +4 Production from all Great Works.
      - Empiricism (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * +1 Food for every Citizen and +25% Growth in all Cities.
        - * +3% Science in a City for every Great Work present (up to 20%).
      - Iron Curtain (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Order): Iron Curtain: Free Courthouse upon immediate City annexation. +200% Food or Production from Internal Trade Routes. City connections generate +5 Gold and Production.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Patriotic War (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Patriotic War: Land Units gain +20% Strength when in or adjacent to a City. When you conquer a City for the first time, all Combat Units within 3 Tiles of the City are fully healed. Can build T-34s.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Exchange Markets (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +1 Trade Route.
        - * +1 Happiness for every active Trade Route.
        - * +15% Tourism modifier for Trade Routes.
        - * Resources from City-States count towards Global Monopolies.
      - Ceremony (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * +25% Production toward National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * Royal Astrologer built in Capital (+3 Science, +1 Science to all Councils, Smokehouses, and Herbalists; 1 Scientist Slot).
      - Justice (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - * Cities with a garrison gain +25% Ranged Combat Strength.
        - * Royal Guardhouse built in Capital (+3 Production, +2 Defense, +50 Hit Points, +10% Military Supply from Population in all Cities, 1 Engineer Slot).
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

    - #0:
      - role: user
      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 10
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 8
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 10
        - Boldness: 9
        - WarBias: 9
        - HostileBias: 9
        - WarmongerHate: 10
        - NeutralBias: 1
        - FriendlyBias: 9
        - GuardedBias: 10
        - AfraidBias: 1
        - DiplomaticBalance: 10
        - Friendliness: 10
        - WorkWithWillingness: 10
        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 10
        - Loyalty: 10
        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 10
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 1
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 1
        - MinorCivWarBias: 1
        - DenounceWillingness: 10
        - Forgiveness: 1
        - Meanness: 8
        - Neediness: 9
        - Chattiness: 9
        - DeceptiveBias: 5
        - Rationale: India continues its overwhelming military build-up and vassalization of Sweden. Increase WarBias and HostileBias to ensure our AI treats India with maximum aggression and rejects peace. Increase Neediness to reflect urgent requirement for allied support from Brazil and Mongolia against the Indian coalition. This is a total war scenario, requiring maximal focus on military coordination and hostility towards the enemy coalition.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Radar
        - Rationale: India's nuclear strike on Honolulu and continued air/naval superiority demands immediate air power. Radar unlocks Fighter and Heavy Bomber for air defense and offensive strikes. Telecommunications can wait; survival requires modern air units to counter Indian carriers, B17s, and XCOM squads.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Double Agents (Policy)
        - Rationale: India's espionage activity remains high. This policy (available in 10 turns) provides 200 Spy Points and Science when identifying foreign spies, enhancing our counterintelligence to protect our investments and yields. Synergizes with our max Espionage flavor. Given the intense conflict, denying intelligence to India and gaining Science is more critical than short-term economic or military policies.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### The Celts
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: The Celts are India's vassal and at war with us. Keeping private hostility at -100 ensures our AI treats them as an extension of India's coalition.
        - UpdatedTurn: 286
        
        ### Polynesia
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -80
        - Rationale: Polynesia is at war with us, has denounced us, and is firmly aligned with India via defensive pacts. Their navy is active near our coastal zones. Keeping private relationship at -80 ensures our AI prioritizes military actions against them, focuses on naval and land engagements, and resists peace overtures unless strategically advantageous.
        - UpdatedTurn: 295
        
        ### Kwa-Bulawayo
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 25
        - Rationale: Kwa-Bulawayo is under direct naval assault from India (suffering city bombardment). Increasing private relationship to 25 (from 20) further encourages our AI to prioritize naval and military actions to liberate and defend this critical city-state. This adjustment signals urgency for unit gifts, naval support, and liberation efforts to secure our alliance and deny India influence.
        - UpdatedTurn: 296
        
        ### Jakarta
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -50
        - Rationale: Jakarta is at war with us and a key Indian ally. We need to apply military pressure to defend ourselves and eventually flip it. Private -50 signals moderate hostility.
        - UpdatedTurn: 312
        
        ### Warsaw
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Warsaw has declared war on us and is strategically located near our core cities. Maintaining maximum private hostility ensures our AI prioritizes capturing or neutralizing this city-state to secure our borders and deny our enemies a forward base.
        - UpdatedTurn: 324
        
        ### Zurich
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 70
        - Rationale: Zurich is our sphere of influence city-state and under direct threat from Germany and Sweden. Increasing private relationship to 70 ensures our AI strongly defends Zurich, provides military support, and maintains our alliance. This is critical for retaining delegates and securing our core territory.
        - UpdatedTurn: 327
        
        ### Germany
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Germany remains at war with us, allied with Sweden. Their city Dortmund is under siege. Maintain maximum hostility to ensure continued naval bombardment and eventual capture of Dortmund to secure western flank.
        - UpdatedTurn: 341
        
        ### Sweden
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Sweden is actively proposing Decolonization in World Congress (next turn) which would harm our city-state alliances. They are at war with us, allied with Germany and Polynesia, and have 12 delegates (most). Maximum hostility ensures we resist their diplomatic maneuvers and focus military efforts on them. Critical to maintain pressure to reduce their influence.
        - UpdatedTurn: 344
        
        ### India
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: India continues existential threat: they built more Fleet Destroyers, a B17 (upgraded Heavy Bomber), XCOM Squad, and used an Atomic Bomb on Cardiff (Swedish city). Their naval aggression destroyed our Destroyer. We must maintain maximum hostility (-100) to ensure our AI prioritizes defense, rejects peace, and focuses on countering Indian advances. Their tech lead and nuclear capability demand unwavering opposition.
        - UpdatedTurn: 345
        
        ### Antwerp
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Antwerp (City-State ID 30) has declared war on us and is an Indian ally. Set private relationship to -100 to ensure our AI treats it as an enemy and prioritizes military actions against it if it becomes a threat.
        - UpdatedTurn: 346
        
        ### Dorestad
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Dorestad (City-State ID 34) has declared war on us and is an Indian ally. Set private relationship to -100 to ensure our AI treats it as an enemy and focuses on neutralizing it.
        - UpdatedTurn: 346
        
        ### Trowulan
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Trowulan (City-State ID 37) has declared war on us and is an Indian ally. Set private relationship to -100 to ensure our AI treats it as an enemy and resists any peace overtures.
        - UpdatedTurn: 346
        
        ### Msoura
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Msoura (City-State ID 26) has declared war on us and is an Indian ally. Set private relationship to -100 to ensure our AI prioritizes military actions against it.
        - UpdatedTurn: 346
        
        ### Brussels
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Brussels (City-State ID 27) has declared war on us and is an Indian ally. Set private relationship to -100 to ensure our AI treats it as an enemy.
        - UpdatedTurn: 346
        
        ### Mongolia
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Mongolia remains our crucial defensive pact ally and Declaration of Friend. They are engaging India on the northern front. We just renewed an Open Borders deal with them (Turn 347). Maintaining maximum private relationship (100) ensures our AI prioritizes military coordination, joint naval operations, and encourages them to pressure Sweden's northern cities (Turfan, Hsia). Their 4 delegates are vital for World Congress votes against Sweden's Decolonization proposal next turn. We may gift them gold or units to offset their combat penalty unhappiness.
        - UpdatedTurn: 347
        
        ### Brazil
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Brazil is our other critical defensive pact ally. They recaptured Belo Horizonte from India and are actively building naval units (Cruiser, Light Tank). Their military is engaged near Aztlan and our western sea zones. Maximum private relationship (100) ensures our AI coordinates closely with Brazil, shares intelligence, and prioritizes joint naval strikes against Indian/German fleets. Brazil's 8 delegates are essential for World Congress voting. We must strengthen this alliance to withstand India's onslaught.
        - UpdatedTurn: 347
        
        ### Aztlan
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Aztlan is now our allied city-state (re-allied via Great Diplomat). Set private relationship to 100 to ensure our AI prioritizes its defense, sends unit gifts, and maintains the alliance. This secures our western flank and denies enemies a base.
        - UpdatedTurn: 349
        
        ### Cahokia
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Cahokia is now our allied city-state (allied via influence). Previously set to -100 when enemy, now adjust to 100 to encourage military support and defense. This helps secure our northern border against Sweden and India.
        - UpdatedTurn: 349
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: India's nuclear escalation and overwhelming military require maximum defensive posture. Increase Defense to 100 for stronger city garrisons and fortifications. MilitaryTraining to 85 for better unit quality via promotions and upgrades. Recon to 70 for improved scouting of Indian movements. UseNuke to 95 to reflect readiness for nuclear retaliation if attacked with WMDs. Maintain high Air, Antiair, Mobilization, and CityDefense to withstand Indian assaults.
        - GrandStrategy: UnitedNations

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 43
        - Status: World Congress - Voting in 1 turn
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Scholars In Residence:
            - Description: Civilizations have -20% Research cost for Technologies already discovered by another Civilization. Additional -2% for every City-State Ally, up to -50%.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Travel Ban:
            - Description: Restricts international travel between Civilizations. Reduces the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 35%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Babylonian Sphere of Influence: Zurich:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - World Religion: Buddhism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: -1
        - Proposals:

            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.

        You, Nebuchadnezzar II (leader of Babylon, Player 6), are making strategic decisions after turn 353.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Nebuchadnezzar II, leader of Babylon (Opponent 6).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Babylon
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Bowman, Replacing Archer (Requires technology: Calendar)
        - Unique Unit: Sabum Kibitum, Replacing Spearman (Requires technology: Bronze Working)
        - Unique Building: Walls of Babylon, Replacing Walls (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Unique Building: Etemenanki, Replacing National Monument (Requires technology: Drama and Poetry)
        - Civilization Ability: Receive a Free Great Scientist when you discover Writing, and Great Scientists are earned 50% faster than normal. Investing Gold in Buildings reduces their Production cost by an additional 15%.
      - Leader: Nebuchadnezzar II
      - OpponentID: 6
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Satellites: Allow you to build the Apollo Program, a project necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: The Internet, Lasers
      - Radar: Allows you to build the Heavy Bomber, a deadly air Unit capable of delivering death from afar, and the Fighter, an air Unit designed to wrest control of the skies from enemy aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Advanced Ballistics, Mobile Tactics
      - Alternative Energy: Allows you to build the Solar Plant, a building which may only be built in Cities on or next to Desert, and increases Production. Also allows you to build the Recycling Center, which provides bonus Aluminum.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics
      
      ## Policies
      - Spaceflight Pioneers (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Order): Spaceflight Pioneers: Gain a free Great Engineer and Great Scientist. May finish Spaceship parts with Great Engineers, and expending a Great Person grants 100 Science, scaling with Era. Hurrying Production with Great Engineers 50% more effective.
      - Socialist Realism (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Order): Socialist Realism: Unlocks the Propaganda Process (converts 15% of Production into Tourism). +25% Tourism to other Civilizations following Order. +4 Production from all Great Works.
      - Empiricism (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * +1 Food for every Citizen and +25% Growth in all Cities.
        - * +3% Science in a City for every Great Work present (up to 20%).
      - Iron Curtain (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Order): Iron Curtain: Free Courthouse upon immediate City annexation. +200% Food or Production from Internal Trade Routes. City connections generate +5 Gold and Production.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Patriotic War (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Patriotic War: Land Units gain +20% Strength when in or adjacent to a City. When you conquer a City for the first time, all Combat Units within 3 Tiles of the City are fully healed. Can build T-34s.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Exchange Markets (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +1 Trade Route.
        - * +1 Happiness for every active Trade Route.
        - * +15% Tourism modifier for Trade Routes.
        - * Resources from City-States count towards Global Monopolies.
      - Ceremony (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * +25% Production toward National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * Royal Astrologer built in Capital (+3 Science, +1 Science to all Councils, Smokehouses, and Herbalists; 1 Scientist Slot).
      - Justice (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - * Cities with a garrison gain +25% Ranged Combat Strength.
        - * Royal Guardhouse built in Capital (+3 Production, +2 Defense, +50 Hit Points, +10% Military Supply from Population in all Cities, 1 Engineer Slot).
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Rationale: India continues its aggressive military expansion, having just captured Honolulu, and fields advanced B17 bombers. To secure our airspace, coastal cities, and fleet, we must prioritize Radar as our next technology. This will unlock Fighters for interception and air superiority, along with Heavy Bombers for offensive counter-strikes, immediately after we complete our current research on Telecommunications.
        - Technology: Radar
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Policy: Patriotic War
        - Rationale: With India deploying XCOM Squads and advanced forces near Nantes, and our peace treaty with Germany expiring in two turns, our immediate survival hinges on city defense. Patriotic War provides a crucial +20% combat strength to land units when in or adjacent to our cities. This defensive bonus will significantly enhance our ability to hold key border cities like Dortmund, Warsaw, and Shushan against overwhelming enemy numbers.
    - #2:
      - toolName: keep-status-quo
      - rationale: Our current grand strategy (United Nations) and highly focused flavors are perfectly tailored for this total war scenario. We have prioritized military defense, naval/air supremacy, science, and diplomacy at maximum levels (100) to counter India's massive military advantage and protect our critical city-state alliances. Strategic nuclear readiness (95) is also maintained for deterrence. Keeping this status quo ensures our staff continues to execute these critical priorities without dilution.
  - tokens:
    - inputTokens: 55630
    - reasoningTokens: 10105
    - outputTokens: 280
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Assessing Current State**
            
            I am currently analyzing our civilization's status, evaluating potential adversaries, assessing our diplomatic standing, tracking our progress towards victory, and reviewing recent happenings. This comprehensive review is crucial for strategic planning.
            
            **Revising Research Strategy**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our technology path, as Telecommunications was researched instead of Radar. I need to confirm our next research priority from Satellites, Radar, or Alternative Energy, given our diplomatic victory objective and current vote count in the World Congress.
            
            **Analyzing Diplomatic Threats**
            
            India's overwhelming delegate count is alarming; they're a mere 16 delegates from a Diplomatic Victory. Their aggressive expansion, evidenced by capturing Honolulu and sinking our Dreadnought, also poses significant threats across all victory paths. Their current military presence in Celtic territory is concerning.
            
            **Reviewing Policy Adoption**
            
            I'm reviewing our policy adoption strategy now. It seems "Double Agents" was our previous choice, but I'm assessing if we have sufficient culture to adopt it, especially considering the 8 turns remaining. I need to confirm the optimal policy from the extensive list of available options.
            
            **Prioritizing Future Research**
            
            I'm reconfirming our research direction. Despite previous plans, Telecommunications is still the current focus. I've decided to prioritize Radar next to counter India's air superiority, but I will also evaluate Satellites and Alternative Energy as potential alternatives given our diplomatic victory aim and the World Congress situation.
            
            **Assessing Geopolitical Alignments**
            
            I'm reassessing our diplomatic landscape. Germany's expiring peace treaty is a concern, especially with their ally Sweden now capitulated to India, alongside the Celts. Polynesia, already at war with us, is also being demolished by India. This shift in power dynamics requires immediate attention.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Ties**
            
            I'm reviewing our current city-state relationships and private diplomatic adjustments. While most seem stable, I'm questioning our continued hostility towards Polynesia, given their dire situation against India. It's worth considering if shifting our stance could better serve our long-term strategic goals.
            
            **Evaluating Diplomatic Maneuvers**
            
            I'm now considering shifting my focus from Polynesia back to Germany and our expiring peace treaty. Their alliance with the now Indian vassal Sweden is a significant concern, and we've previously denounced each other, complicating any immediate détente. I need to assess how to navigate this impending conflict.
            
            **Analyzing Diplomatic Votes**
            
            I'm now analyzing the upcoming World Congress vote, which is only one turn away. I need to carefully consider Sweden's proposal to repeal Casus Belli and Germany's proposal regarding Kyiv, as these could significantly impact our diplomatic and military standing. I am also evaluating our options in light of our strained relationship with Germany.
            
            **Preventing Cultural Victory**
            
            I am now intensely focused on preventing India's imminent Cultural Victory by ensuring the "Travel Ban" remains active. This requires influencing our tactical staff's vote against its repeal, a critical diplomatic maneuver given India's near-certain victory otherwise.
            
            **Refining Persona Parameters**
            
            I am now meticulously adjusting our internal persona values. My current focus is on recalibrating growth and expansion flavors, while ensuring our strong military and diplomatic leanings remain intact, especially in light of recent geopolitical shifts.
            
            **Adjusting Research Focus**
            
            My current thoughts are on refining our research trajectory. While I was previously set on Radar, the discovery that our core cities are contributing to the International Space Station project is a significant development. This shift in focus necessitates a re-evaluation of our technological priorities.
            
            **Completing City Improvements**
            
            I'm focused on finalizing civic projects in Shushan and our core cities, nearing completion. My immediate concern is Mari's severe unhappiness; I'm investigating the -11 happiness delta there, noting its coastal position and proximity to the front lines, alongside similar issues in Shushan, Hyderabad, Warsaw, and Dortmund, despite an overall empire happiness of 51%.
            
            **Evaluating Combat Weaknesses**
            
            My analysis reveals a significant deficit in land unit strength against India's elite forces. I am prioritizing advancements that will counter their superior Machine Guns and XCOM Squads. I'm also reassessing our naval positioning and unit composition to address immediate threats.
            
            **Analyzing Naval Engagements**
            
            I'm assessing our recent naval engagements and unit upgrades. Our strong fleet, featuring multiple Dreadnoughts and Battleships, proved effective against India's fleet destroyer. I've also upgraded a Destroyer to a Fleet Destroyer and am now reviewing city-state relationships and potential modifier adjustments.
            
            **Assessing Diplomatic Alliances**
            
            I'm reassessing our strategic alliances with city-states. Cahokia's alliance with Germany presents a potential threat should war re-ignite. I'm weighing whether to antagonize them now or focus on securing a future alliance, given Germany's overwhelming influence there.
            
            **Breaking India's City-State Grip**
            
            I'm strategizing to dismantle India's overwhelming control over city-states. With most aligned against us and at war, direct influence is impossible. I'm evaluating options like capturing cities, using spies for elections, or defeating India, while simultaneously redeploying agents to break their delegate advantage.
            
            **Revising Victory Strategy**
            
            I'm reconsidering our endgame. While the United Nations offers a diplomatic victory, I'm weighing if a Spaceship victory might be more advantageous given Babylon's scientific bonuses. This requires a deeper dive into our current science output and projections.
            
            **Prioritizing Science Focus**
            
            I'm now weighing our victory paths, recognizing India's technological lead but our potential to catch up through focused science for a Spaceship victory. Diplomatic victory remains strong, so I'm prioritizing securing city-state alliances and blocking India's impending win, while simultaneously building science to counter their offensive capabilities.
            
            **Adjusting Strategy Priorities**
            
            I'm reassessing our strategic direction given our overwhelming military superiority over Germany. My focus is now on optimizing our already excellent flavors, which align perfectly with our goals of total war, defense, and high technology, by calling `keep-status-quo`. I'm also reviewing our diplomatic relationships, specifically with Germany, whose peace treaty expires in two turns.
            
            **Defending Against Giants**
            
            I'm reassessing our military's true strength. While we dominate Germany, Sweden, and the Celts, India's might is overwhelming. All efforts must now focus on defending against India, avoiding any conflicts that might distract us from this primary threat, especially with Germany's expiring peace treaty and their problematic alliances.
            
            **Securing Western Borders**
            
            I've confirmed we successfully captured Dortmund from Germany, explaining their animosity and the previous advantageous peace treaty. Building a castle there now to solidify our hold. My analysis of Sweden's occupied cities suggests they captured them from Mongolia, whom we allied with. Sweden, as India's vassal, is now warring with our ally Mongolia, and I've set their relationship to a private -100.
            
            **Analyzing World Congress**
            
            I am currently focused on the impending World Congress vote, specifically assessing the delegate distribution between the Indian coalition and our own. India's significant advantage necessitates a review of potential allies and strategies to counter their proposals, particularly the repeal of Travel Ban.
            
            **Guiding Diplomatic Staff**
            
            I'm ensuring our diplomatic staff votes in our favor, leveraging high Diplomacy flavor and negative relationships with India and Sweden to oppose their key proposals. I'm also confirming our strong alliances with Brazil and Mongolia, and assessing our strategic relationship with Cahokia, considering Germany's influence, to best position us for future diplomatic gains.
            
            **Prioritizing Research Decisions**
            
            I'm solidifying our research path towards Radar, recognizing its critical role in countering India's air superiority with Fighters and Heavy Bombers. I'm also re-evaluating city-state relationships, particularly ensuring Zurich and Aztlan are secured at maximum influence, while confirming our adversarial stance against Indian allies.
            
            **Setting Research and Policy**
            
            I'm confirming Radar as our next research, vital for countering India's bombers with Fighters. Additionally, I've decided "Double Agents" is our next policy, as its spy-related bonuses are crucial given recent espionage attempts by Germany and India.
            
            **Prioritizing Defensive Policies**
            
            I'm now focused on selecting our next policy. Given the existential threat from India's overwhelming military, a +20% combat strength bonus near cities, like "Patriotic War," seems far more critical for survival than enhanced spy protection, especially since India is already technologically ahead of us.
            
            **Choosing Defensive Policies**
            
            My current thoughts are on selecting the optimal policy given the escalating military threat. While "Double Agents" offers espionage benefits, the immediate need for survival against India's advanced units, evidenced by their recent captures, makes "Patriotic War" and its defensive combat bonus a more critical choice for protecting our cities.
            
            **Selecting Defensive Policy**
            
            I am selecting "Patriotic War" as our next policy. This will grant a significant combat bonus to our land units defending our core cities, which is paramount given India's overwhelming military superiority. I have also confirmed our flavors are aligned and will maintain the status quo.
            
            **Refining Diplomatic Flavors**
            
            My current thoughts focus on finalizing our diplomatic and military flavors, which I've determined are exceptionally well-aligned with our objectives. Therefore, I'm choosing to maintain the status quo, which aligns perfectly with our goals of total war, defense, and high technology. I've also noted that we cannot use `set-relationship` for city-states.
            
            **Confirming Persona Values**
            
            My current focus is on confirming our persona settings. I've reviewed our relationships with major civilizations and determined they are optimally set, negating the need for `set-relationship` calls. Our persona values are also perfectly aligned, so I will maintain the status quo for `set-persona`.
            
            **Finalizing Strategic Actions**
            
            I have confirmed our persona values are optimal for this war scenario, so no changes are needed there. My immediate focus is on executing the finalized strategic actions: setting research to **Radar** for air superiority, selecting **Patriotic War** for city defense against India's advanced units, and maintaining our current status quo for all other parameters.
            
            **Finalizing Actions**
            
            I've confirmed our persona settings are optimal, so I'm maintaining the status quo. My immediate focus is on executing our finalized strategic actions: setting research to Radar for air superiority, selecting Patriotic War for city defense against India's advanced units, and keeping our current grand strategy and flavors.

          - toolName: set-policy
          - input:
            - Policy: Patriotic War
            - Rationale: With India deploying XCOM Squads and advanced forces near Nantes, and our peace treaty with Germany expiring in two turns, our immediate survival hinges on city defense. Patriotic War provides a crucial +20% combat strength to land units when in or adjacent to our cities. This defensive bonus will significantly enhance our ability to hold key border cities like Dortmund, Warsaw, and Shushan against overwhelming enemy numbers.

          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #3:

          - toolName: keep-status-quo

            - Rationale: Our current grand strategy (United Nations) and highly focused flavors are perfectly tailored for this total war scenario. We have prioritized military defense, naval/air supremacy, science, and diplomacy at maximum levels (100) to counter India's massive military advantage and protect our critical city-state alliances. Strategic nuclear readiness (95) is also maintained for deterrence. Keeping this status quo ensures our staff continues to execute these critical priorities without dilution.

          - providerOptions: undefined
